# Bug audit — 2026-10-04

The area is the `packages/ui` components that start with C. 7 scopes, 118 files, 10,425 lines, and 150 claims. 137 findings stay open; 13 claims are ruled out.

This record replaces the paused record of commit a745b7f1a and its checkpoint of commit 0d0a2d5bb.

## Scope

The area holds Calendar, Card, Checkbox, Code, Collapse, Color (ColorPanel, ColorPicker, and their parts), Combobox, CommandPalette, Confirm, ContextMenu, Control, CopyButton, CreditCardInput, and CurrencyInput, each with the kata recipe it reads. It leaves out the `chart` and `chat` modules and the `container` structure, which are not components; the tests and the benchmarks; and the demo tree, which the verifier reads as a consumer root. A file outside the area that a claim leads to is under **Surfaced, not judged**.

| Scope | Theme | Files | Lines | Claims |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | Calendar | 17 | 2,084 | 14 |
| C2 | Combobox | 14 | 1,974 | 27 |
| C3 | Color: ColorPanel, ColorPicker, and their parts | 22 | 1,910 | 18 |
| C4 | CommandPalette, Confirm, and ContextMenu | 16 | 1,311 | 26 |
| C5 | Card, Code, and CopyButton | 19 | 1,057 | 20 |
| C6 | Checkbox, Collapse, and Control | 20 | 1,052 | 12 |
| C7 | CreditCardInput and CurrencyInput | 10 | 1,037 | 33 |
| Total | | 118 | 10,425 | 150 |

The intent sources are `CONVENTIONS.md` §3.6, §3.9, §7.2, §7.3, and §11.3 (and §10.7 for C2), `packages/ui/REFERENCE.md` §2, and the TSDoc, comments, and tests of each scope. The consumer roots are `apps/admin`, `apps/places`, `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos` (now `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos`, see **Method**), `packages/ui/src/modules`, `packages/ui/src/layouts`, and the shipped defaults of each component; `packages/ui/src/__tests__` is a weaker root.

## Method

One `bug-sweeper` read each scope and returned claims with an evidence block. Each coverage line matched its list: every file of every scope opened and was read to the last line. The caller stripped the evidence from each claim by code, and one `bug-verifier` judged each scope blind, with the file list, the intent sources, the consumer roots, and the prior-art digest. No claim came back UNLOCATED, so none went out again. An overturn pass for each scope then read the sheet with the sweep evidence and the caller checks, and one merge pass joined the groups across the scopes and ordered the steps.

The prior-art digest held the open rows, the ruled-out entries, and the surfaced entries of the open audits that cover the area: DOCS-SITE A15, D37, D42, and D49 and three entries of its §9; IOS-TOUCH-HOLD S2, S3, and S4. The CLEANUP audit has no open entry in the area, and the CHART audit covers only the chart module. No row of the area matches a digest entry.

| Pass | In | Out |
|---|---|---|
| Sweep | 7 scopes, 118 files | 150 claims; 26 leads outside the area |
| Blind verify | 150 claims, stripped by code to id, file, symbol, trigger, wrong result, and contract | 120 CONFIRMED, 13 NARROWED, 1 RESTATED, 16 REFUTED, 0 UNLOCATED |
| Re-issue | 0 UNLOCATED claims | not run |
| Overturn | 7 sheets, the sweep evidence, the caller checks | 7 rows changed (C1-C03, C3-C05, C3-C18, C4-C25, C7-C08, C7-C18, C7-C21); fields amended on C4-C13, C5-C12, C5-C15, C6-C02, C6-C11. Final: 117 CONFIRMED, 19 NARROWED, 1 RESTATED, 13 REFUTED |
| Merge | 7 sheets | 26 groups, 100 steps, 60 questions |
| Settle | the answers of the owner to 52 questions, word for word | 48 steps re-derived; 7 questions back to the owner; steps still blocked: S09, S15, S22, S35, S42, S44, S49, S50, S54, S55, S64, S73, S74, S76, S93, S94, S99; 2 new questions (Q61, Q62) |

The owner answered on 2026-10-05: "Go ahead as recommended for the items that are straightforward or you are confident it is a good pattern and/or fixing a bug that there isn't a better way to solve. Any items you aren't confident of, re-surface to me or do a spike." The caller kept seven questions open for the owner (Q01, Q25, Q27, Q39, Q52, Q56, Q58) and settled Q21 with a spike in the `motion` browser project: with no guard, a double click on the confirm button ran `onConfirm` twice; `pointer-events: none` on the Overlay root still ran it twice, because the dialog panel sets its own `pointer-events-auto`; `inert` on the root ran it once.

After the passes, #1926 moved the old docs app, its demos, and its engine from `packages/ui/src/docs` to `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy`, and started a new docs app in `packages/ui/src/docs`. The move touched no scope file. This record writes each path of the old tree at its new place, so a demo that a reach answer cites is under `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos`. The legacy app stays as a reference until the rewrite is complete; the verifiers judged reach while it was the docs site. Line numbers read against `main` at b9e37b552, the base of the passes.

A pause and a restart broke the run twice. The owner paused the audit after the sweeps; the sweep claims sat in the paused record, which left the tree before the blind pass so that no verifier could read it. A container restart later stopped the first overturn passes before they reported, and all seven ran again from the same inputs.

### Caller checks

A Chromium probe ran under the `packages/ui` Vitest browser config, and Node 22 with ICU 77.1 read the locale data. A cell that names only the engine means that the probe reproduced the claim. The overturn passes received those cells as written and read a bare "Chromium" as a check with no outcome; a reproduction cannot show a claim deliberate or known, so no verdict rests on that reading. Three first probes missed their claims: the C6-C05 and C6-C06 probes read the overlay input, not the visible box, and the C2-C19 probe ran in the project that mocks `@floating-ui/react`. Each ran again on the right subject and reproduced its claim.

| Row | Check |
|---|---|
| C1-C07 | Chromium: 34 Tab presses cross one calendar |
| C1-C08 | Chromium |
| C1-C14 | Chromium: the RTL start keeps its band-side corners round |
| C2-C19 | Chromium, rerun in the `floating-ui` project (live floating-ui, a trusted CDP key from `vitest/browser`, `IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT` false, focus on the combobox input, a Drawer whose `onOpenChange` writes state): one Escape closed the panel and the Drawer (`onOpenChange(false)` fired once), with and without a typed query. Reproduced. The first probe ran in the mocked `browser` project and did not reproduce. |

| C6-C06 | Chromium, rerun on the box `[data-slot="control"]`: a checked blue box at rest has border `oklab(0.424 -0.0151 -0.1984 / 0.9)`; a box hover or a label hover turns it to `oklab(0.141 … / 0.7)` in light mode and `oklab(0.99999 … / 0.5)` in dark mode. The first probe read the overlay input, which has no border rule, and did not reproduce. |

## Findings

By severity: 1 high, 15 medium, 121 low. By reach: 60 shipped, 12 test-only, 65 none.

**Status:** ◯ OPEN is a finding with no fix; ◐ FIXED is fixed on a branch; ✅ RESOLVED cites the pull request that closed it; a dash marks a row the owner closed by decision. Each cell is copied from the mechanism entry of its row.

## Mechanisms

Each entry is the record of the blind verifier for the row, with the amendments of the overturn pass applied and the overturn entry quoted under **Overturn pass**. The entry is the specification of the fix. Paths are repository-relative, and a line reference such as `:42` reads against the last path before it. In C7, "(P)" names the prior-art search that the C7 sheet ran once for every row, over every digest entry; "R" and "R2" name its resolution notes: no override, patch, path, alias, or vendored copy applies to `card-validator`, `credit-card-type`, `jsdom`, or `@testing-library/user-event`, so the build resolves the store copies cited. In the C2 overturn entries, "IN" is the overturn input.

### C1: Calendar

#### C1-C01

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-range.tsx`
- **Symbol:** `CalendarRange` (`computeRangeDayFlags`, `getDayProps`)
- **Verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **Mechanism:**
  - The preview becomes the effective end. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-range.tsx:150` `const effectiveEnd = hoverDate ?? rangeEnd`
  - With no start, that end still counts as an edge. `:66-68` `const isEdge =` … `(effectiveEnd != null && isSameDay(date, effectiveEnd))`
  - The early return keeps it. `:70-71` `if (rangeStart == null || effectiveEnd == null) {` / `return { isEdge, isInnerRange: false, isLeftEdge: false, isRightEdge: false }`
  - `getDayProps` selects it. `:163-165` `selected: isEdge,` / `variant: isInnerRange ? 'soft' : undefined,` / `color: isInnerRange ? 'blue' : undefined,`
  - The grid takes that flag. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-grid.tsx:95` `const selected = dayProps?.selected ?? isSelected`
  - The cell paints it as committed:
    - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-day-cell.tsx:61` `aria-selected={selected}`
    - `:64` `variant={variant ?? (selected ? 'solid' : isToday ? 'soft' : 'plain')}`
    - `:65` `color={color ?? (selected || isToday ? 'blue' : undefined)}`
  - The listbox is multiselectable. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-range.tsx:189` `multiselectable` → `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-grid.tsx:74` `aria-multiselectable={ariaAttr(multiselectable)}`
- **Trigger:** As stated.
- **Documented intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-range.tsx:36` "Day under the pointer, used as the provisional end of the band before the second click commits `rangeEnd`." This scopes the prop to the time between the two clicks. It says nothing about a preview with no start.
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-range.test.tsx:90-93` pins a selected hover end only when a start exists: `renderUI(<CalendarRange rangeStart={d(2024, 3, 5)} hoverDate={d(2024, 3, 8)} />)` / `expect(findDay(8)).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true')`.
  - No source makes the no-start case deliberate.
- **Reach:** none — NONE.
  - No root calls `CalendarRange` directly (search `\bCalendar(Range|Skeleton)?\b` in apps, demos, modules, layouts).
  - The only range route is `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/components/filters.tsx:136` `<DatePicker range />`. DatePicker is out-of-root and never sends a preview without a start:
    - `packages/ui/src/components/date-picker/use-date-picker-range-state.ts:262` `rangeStart: rangeStart ?? (value ? value[0] : null),`
    - `:264` `hoverDate: rangeStart !== null ? hoverDate : null,`
- **Severity:** low. No shipped surface builds this state, and the cost is one day announced as selected while the pointer rests on it.
- **Group:** independent
- **Prior art:** NONE (terms: CalendarRange, hover, hoverDate, aria-selected, range).
- **Dependency:** none (no node_modules citation).
- **Overturn pass:** **STANDS.** The parent drops a hover that comes before the first endpoint: `packages/ui/src/components/date-picker/date-picker-range-reducer.ts:52` "A hover before the first endpoint shows no band, and a hover on the" and `:55` `if (state.rangeStart === null || sameHoverDate(state.hoverDate, action.date)) return state`. That supports reach NONE and the parent's-duty arm of Q3, but nothing in CalendarRange makes the case deliberate. `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-range.test.tsx:108` covers a lone `rangeEnd` for the month view only.

#### C1-C02

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-skeleton.tsx`
- **Symbol:** `CalendarSkeleton`
- **Verdict:** NARROWED
- **Mechanism:**
  - With no `size`, the skeleton writes no scope and takes the nearest one. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-skeleton.tsx:32` `<div data-density={size} aria-hidden="true" className={cn(k.base, 'relative', className)}>`
  - Its header row and day rows use the button height:
    - `:35` `<div className={cn(k.header, k.skeleton.row)} />`
    - `:43` `<div key={rowKey} className={cn('col-span-7', k.skeleton.row)} />`
    - Chain: `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/calendar.ts:63` `skeleton: kokkaku.calendar,` → `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/calendar.ts:22` `row: button.height,` → `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/button.ts:18` `const height = dan.size.button.base` → `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/dan/size.ts:67` `base: 'density-h-[5.5,7.5,9.5,11.5,11.5]',`
    - That is 22px at xs and 30px at sm.
  - The width and the header margin are three-value ramps, so xs equals sm:
    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/dan/size.ts:144` `width: 'density-w-[52,68,80]',`
    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/dan/space.ts:185` `bottom: 'density-mb-[1,2,3]',`
    - `packages/ui/src/core/density/steps.ts:109` `return { xs: sm, sm, md, lg, xl: lg }`
  - The weekday row is square on the column width, so it matches. `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/calendar.ts:34` `'w-full aspect-square',`
  - The calendar snaps xs to sm:
    - The scale has no xs step: `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/calendar.ts:67-72` builds it from four three-value ramps.
    - `packages/ui/src/core/density/scale.ts:96` `return (scale as readonly DensityStep[]).includes(step) ? (step as S) : (toInnerStep(step) as S)`
    - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx:192` `const step = useDensityStep(size)`
    - `:196` `const density = snapped === step ? size : snapped`
    - `:289` `<Box data-slot="calendar" density={density} …>`
    - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar.test.tsx:288-296` pins the sm scope inside `<Box density="xs">`.
  - Result: seven rows (header plus six day rows) × 8px = 56px.
- **Trigger:** (verified) An xs scope opened through a `density` prop (for example `<Box density="xs">`) around a Suspense fallback or a `loading.tsx` that shows `<CalendarSkeleton />` with no `size`. It resolves to `<Calendar />` showing a six-week month.
  - A client conditional render also shifts. But `CONVENTIONS.md:41` directs that swap to ReadyReveal: "When the client swaps a skeleton for the real component, use `ReadyReveal`, not a conditional render."
  - Three routes are ruled out (see the sheet): ReadyReveal, a root step of xs, and a raw `data-density` attribute.
- **Documented intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-skeleton.tsx:23-24` "six day rows come from the calendar recipes, so the block has the box of a" / "real calendar at each step."
  - `packages/ui/REFERENCE.md:32` "A skeleton follows density as its component does."
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/geometry/skeleton-parity.test.tsx:797` `it.each(['sm', 'md', 'lg'] as const)('CalendarSkeleton has the box of a %s calendar', (size) => {` covers explicit steps only.
  - Nothing makes the xs gap deliberate.
- **Reach:** test-only — NONE.
  - The one root site, `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/primitives/skeleton.tsx:65` `{ name: 'Calendar', skeleton: <CalendarSkeleton /> },`, renders alone (`:140` `{active?.skeleton}`). It has no xs scope and no swap.
  - No app renders `CalendarSkeleton`.
  - Test-only sites: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/a11y/cases/inputs.tsx:244` and `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/geometry/skeleton-parity.test.tsx:801`.
- **Severity:** low. No shipped surface puts the skeleton in an xs scope, and the cost is one 56px shift at a server swap.
- **Group:** independent
- **Prior art:** NONE (terms: skeleton, density, xs, ReadyReveal, layout shift).
- **Overturn pass:** **STANDS.**
  - Two sources state that the skeleton matches the calendar: `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/calendar.ts:9-10` "…so the silhouette takes the step of its nearest density / scope, as the calendar does.", and `packages/ui/docs/COMPONENTS.md:80` "It has the box of the real component…".
  - `CONVENTIONS.md:43` "It reads no context" limits how the fix can work. It does not make the gap deliberate.
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/helpers/size-axes.ts:21` `CalendarSkeleton: ['sm', 'md', 'lg'],` covers no xs scope.
  - The sweep's ReadyReveal and root-xs routes stay ruled out: `packages/ui/src/primitives/ready-reveal/ready-reveal.tsx:111-112`, and `packages/ui/src/core/density/steps.ts:5-7`.

#### C1-C03

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts`
- **Symbol:** `fromCalendarDate`, and through it `firstOfMonth`, `getCalendarDays`, `getWeekdayLabels`, `getMonthLabels`
- **Verdict:** NARROWED
- **Mechanism:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:45-46` `export function fromCalendarDate(date: CalendarDate): Date {` / `return date.toDate(getLocalTimeZone())`
  - `toDate` hands off to the conversion module:
    - `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/dist/private/CalendarDate.mjs:81-82` `toDate(timeZone) {` / `return (0, $d07e34cce18680fd$export$e67a095c620b86fe)(this, timeZone);`
    - `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/dist/private/conversion.mjs:138-139` `…(dateTime, timeZone, disambiguation = 'compatible') {` / `return new Date($d07e34cce18680fd$export$5107c82f94518f5c(dateTime, timeZone, disambiguation));`
  - A UTC zone returns UTC midnight. `conversion.mjs:99` `if (timeZone === 'UTC') return $d07e34cce18680fd$export$bd4fb2bc8bb06fb(dateTime);`
  - An override skips the native path. `conversion.mjs:101-102` "Skip this fast path if the local timezone was explicitly overridden via setLocalTimeZone, since native Date always uses the browser's timezone, not the overridden one."
  - The setter records the override. `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/dist/private/queries.mjs:95-97` `function $ad063034c8620db8$export$61a9d83ceb59a3dd(timeZone) {` / `$ad063034c8620db8$var$localTimeZoneOverride = true;` / `$ad063034c8620db8$var$localTimeZone = timeZone;`
  - Every read in the calendar uses the runtime zone's local getters:
    - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-month.ts:17` `return firstOfMonth(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth())`
    - `:50` `const index = month.getFullYear() * 12 + month.getMonth() + delta`
    - `:102-104` year and month come from `viewDate.getFullYear()` / `viewDate.getMonth()`
  - Traced in America/New_York after `setLocalTimeZone('UTC')`:
    - June seeds `firstOfMonth(2025, 5)` = 2025-06-01T00:00Z = May 31, 20:00 local. The view is May, and the header reads "May 2025" (`packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:144` formats in the runtime zone).
    - The cells run Apr 30 to May 30, so the `getDate() === 1` column offset (`packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-grid.tsx:97`) lands on the second cell.
    - `nextMonth` gives the same instant, so the view does not move. `prevMonth` gives Mar 31 local, so it lands on March.
    - `:164` `formatter.format(firstOfMonth(2021, index))` gives "Dec, Jan, …".
    - `:154` gives a weekday row that starts on Sat.
  - Every bullet of the stated wrong result traces.
- **Trigger:** (verified, as amended by the overturn pass) - (1) As in the sheet: an app calls `setLocalTimeZone` with a zone east of the runtime zone, then renders `<Calendar />`.
  - (2) Restored in part: the runtime zone is exactly `UTC` when the library first reads it, and the OS zone then moves to a zone west of UTC while the tab stays open. Each later `fromCalendarDate` returns UTC midnight, which is the evening before in the new zone, and every bullet of the C03 trace follows.
  - From any other first zone, `:103` takes the native path, and the sheet's drop holds.
- **Documented intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:38` "Inverse of `toCalendarDate`: a local-midnight `Date` for the calendar day."
  - `:27-28` "This mirrors the wall-clock-day semantics the calendar uses everywhere (`getFullYear`/`getMonth`/`getDate`)".
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-utilities.test.ts:71-77` asserts local midnight with no override.
  - Not deliberate.
- **Reach:** shipped —
  - (1) NONE, as in the sheet.
  - (2) No consumer builds this trigger; the shipped default reaches it. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:46` `return date.toDate(getLocalTimeZone())` runs in every calendar:
    - standalone: `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/components/calendar.tsx:11`, `:34`, `:39`;
    - through DatePicker: `apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-form-drawer.tsx:246` `<DatePicker name="visitedAt" />`, `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/components/date-picker.tsx:14`, `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/components/filters.tsx:136`, `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/providers/glass.tsx:145`, `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/modules/grid/editable.tsx:127`.
  - Not verified in this pass: that a browser moves `Date` to a new OS zone in an open tab.
- **Severity:** low. The level is unchanged; the reason is new. Every calendar can reach (2), but only in a session whose first zone read was UTC and whose zone then moves west, and a reload reads the zone again. Until that reload, every label and both chevrons are wrong.
- **Group:** independent
- **Prior art:** NONE (terms: time zone, getLocalTimeZone, setLocalTimeZone, fromCalendarDate).
- **Dependency:**
  - Package: `@internationalized/date` 3.12.2. `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/package.json:3` `"version": "3.12.2",` and `:22` `"import": "./dist/index.mjs",`.
    - `pnpm-workspace.yaml:47-50`: overrides cover esbuild, postcss, and undici only. `:64-65`: patchedDependencies lists `'@floating-ui/react@0.27.19'` only. None applies.
    - The root package.json has no `pnpm` block. I did not list `patches/`; pnpm applies only the patches it registers.
    - `tsconfig.base.json:19-20` `"paths": {` / `"@/*": ["./src/*"]` — none applies. The packages/ui tsconfig has no paths.
    - No `resolve.alias` in `vitest.config.ts`, `vitest.base.ts`, `vitest.compiler.config.ts`, `vite.fixtures.config.ts`, or `vite.docs.config.ts` (which delegates to `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/engine/vite/index.ts`). The package appears only under `optimizeDeps.include` (`vitest.browser.config.ts:217`, `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/engine/vite/index.ts:107`); that is pre-bundling, not an alias.
    - Vendored copy: I did not search `packages/ui/src`, because that search reaches `__benchmarks__`. The scope imports the bare specifier (`packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:7` `} from '@internationalized/date'`), and no surface maps it, so the build resolves the node_modules copy.
- **Overturn pass:** **CORRECTED. The verdict stays NARROWED, but the dropped OS-zone part is too wide.**
  - **Overturning source:** the guard that the ruled-out entry quotes (`conversion.mjs:103`) never runs when the cached zone is `UTC`.
    - `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/dist/private/conversion.mjs:99` `if (timeZone === 'UTC') return $d07e34cce18680fd$export$bd4fb2bc8bb06fb(dateTime);` returns first.
    - That function builds the instant in UTC: `:31-32` `date.setUTCHours(hour, minute, second, millisecond);` / `date.setUTCFullYear(year, month - 1, day);`.
    - The zone it tests is the first read, kept for the life of the process: `packages/ui/node_modules/@internationalized/date/dist/private/queries.mjs:92` `if ($ad063034c8620db8$var$localTimeZone == null) $ad063034c8620db8$var$localTimeZone = new Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone;`.
    - The suite states the same split for Node: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/modules/chart-time.test.ts:306` `// Node reads a new TZ at once, and the date library keeps its own zone.`
  - **trigger (verified):**
    - (1) As in the sheet: an app calls `setLocalTimeZone` with a zone east of the runtime zone, then renders `<Calendar />`.
    - (2) Restored in part: the runtime zone is exactly `UTC` when the library first reads it, and the OS zone then moves to a zone west of UTC while the tab stays open. Each later `fromCalendarDate` returns UTC midnight, which is the evening before in the new zone, and every bullet of the C03 trace follows.
    - From any other first zone, `:103` takes the native path, and the sheet's drop holds.
  - **reach:**
    - (1) NONE, as in the sheet.
    - (2) No consumer builds this trigger; the shipped default reaches it. `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-utilities.ts:46` `return date.toDate(getLocalTimeZone())` runs in every calendar:
      - standalone: `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/components/calendar.tsx:11`, `:34`, `:39`;
      - through DatePicker: `apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-form-drawer.tsx:246` `<DatePicker name="visitedAt" />`, `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/components/date-picker.tsx:14`, `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/components/filters.tsx:136`, `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/providers/glass.tsx:145`, `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/modules/grid/editable.tsx:127`.
    - Not verified in this pass: that a browser moves `Date` to a new OS zone in an open tab.
  - **severity:** low. The level is unchanged; the reason is new. Every calendar can reach (2), but only in a session whose first zone read was UTC and whose zone then moves west, and a reload reads the zone again. Until that reload, every label and both chevrons are wrong.

- **Trigger:** As stated. For the docs, the build machine's Node default stands in for the server's.
- **Documented intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx:124-126` "Resolution order: explicit prop, then enclosing `LocaleProvider`, then the runtime default." This documents the fallback, not agreement between server and client.
  - `:155-157` covers "today" only.
  - `packages/ui/src/utilities/resolve-locale.ts:6` "while the document's locale is fixed for the process". The server and the browser are two processes.
  - `packages/ui/REFERENCE.md:37` "`locale` needs an API decision — explicit props or app wrappers".
  - The hydration tests render both phases in one process (`packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-hydration.test.tsx:22-48`), so they cannot see this.
  - Not deliberate.
- **Reach:** shipped —
  - The docs site prerenders each page in Node and the browser hydrates it. These files are out-of-root, cited for the render path:
    - `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/react-router.config.ts:20-21` "A static site: no server at run time. The build renders each page to its own" / "HTML file, and the browser hydrates it."

    - `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/app/entry.server.tsx:6` "The build renders each page once, to a static file. `prerender` waits for all content."

    - `:34` `<Axes of="Calendar" omit={['multiselectable']} render={(props) => <Calendar {...props} />} />`
    - `:39` `<Calendar min={min} max={max} />`
  - Apps: NONE. The calendar sits in the portaled DatePicker dialog (`packages/ui/src/components/date-picker/date-picker-content.tsx:85` "Portaled, animated modal dialog shell for the picker popover."), which the server does not render open.
- **Severity:** medium. Every docs reader whose browser locale differs from the build machine's hits it on each calendar demo page. React heals it with a client re-render.

- **Prior art:** NONE (terms: locale, LocaleProvider, hydration, onRecoverableError, prerender).

#### C1-C11

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/use-calendar-month.ts`
- **Symbol:** `useCalendarMonth` (`shown`, seeded `viewDate`)

This won't be patched up". The cells are memoized (`packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar-day-cell.tsx:36` `export const CalendarDayCell = memo(function CalendarDayCell({`), so the server's mark stays.
- **Trigger:** As stated.
- **Documented intent:**

Across a timezone offset at a month boundary, the server and the client can read different months." The guard covers the clock only.

  - Not deliberate.
- **Reach:** none — NONE.

  - The apps mount calendars only in the portaled DatePicker dialog.
- **Severity:** low. No shipped surface server-renders a seed that varies by zone. When hit, the month-boundary case heals by client re-render, but the mid-month case keeps a wrong selected mark.
- **Group:** M03
- **Prior art:** NONE (terms: hydration, time zone, seed, server component).
- **Dependency:** react-dom 19.2.7 (`packages/ui/node_modules/react-dom/package.json:3`). The same surfaces as C03 apply: `pnpm-workspace.yaml:47-50` and `:64-65`, `tsconfig.base.json:19-20`, and no Vite/Vitest alias. None applies. The vendored-copy search was skipped for the same reason.

Neither covers a seed whose day depends on the zone. `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar-hydration.test.tsx:9-13` moves only the clock.

#### C2-C19

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts`
- **Symbol:** useComboboxInput
- **Verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **Mechanism:**
  - The input closes on Escape without preventDefault: `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts:172-175` "if (event.key === 'Escape') {" / "close()" / "" / "return".
  - The combobox's layer exists only while open: `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-escape-layer.ts:59` "if (!open || !enabled || order === null) return" and :63 "const unregister = layered ? registerDismissLayer(layer, order) : undefined", with cleanup at :75-78 "return () => {" / "unsubscribe()" / "" / "unregister?.()".
  - :66-68 "if (event.key !== 'Escape' || event.defaultPrevented) return" / "" / "if (layered && !isTopDismissLayer(layer)) return"
  - `packages/ui/src/utilities/document-listener.ts:42` "for (const h of [...reg.handlers]) {"
  - The surface around it: `packages/ui/src/primitives/overlay/overlay.tsx:147-149` "useDismissable({" / "open," / "onDismiss: () => onOpenChange(false)," → `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-dismissable.ts:55` "useEscapeLayer({"; `packages/ui/src/components/drawer/drawer.tsx:272` "&lt;Overlay".
  - Order of events:
    1. React's root listener runs close().
    2. The browser runs microtasks before the document listener.
    3. React commits open = false and flushes the discrete render's passive effects, so the combobox's layer unregisters.
    4. The document listener now finds the Overlay's layer on top, and closes it.
  - Premise: React 19 flushes a discrete update in a microtask and runs its passive effects at commit, and browsers run microtasks between listeners of a trusted event. jsdom's synthetic dispatch skips that checkpoint, so the jsdom suite cannot show this.
- **Trigger:** as stated (real browser).
- **Documented intent:** `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-escape-layer.ts:28-31` "Escape-key dismissal routed through the shared dismiss-layer stack, so" / "stacked surfaces (menu in dialog, dialog over sheet) close one per press," / "innermost first. Presses a consumer already handled (`preventDefault`)" / "are ignored."
- **Reach:** shipped — apps/places. `apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-form-drawer.tsx:133` "&lt;Drawer" → :205 "&lt;PlaceSearchField />" → `apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-search-field.tsx:54` "&lt;AddressInput" → address-input.tsx:177. The entry is lost because the form re-seeds on each open: `apps/places/src/components/place-form-drawer/place-form-drawer.tsx:158-160` "// The drawer unmounts its children while closed, so the form re-seeds" / "// from `defaultValues` on each open and an abandoned entry never comes" / "// back. …"
- **Severity:** high. A single Escape on open address suggestions closes the add/edit place drawer and discards the entry, on a shipped app path.
- **Group:** M01
- **Prior art:** NONE
- **Overturn pass:** **STANDS.** I weighed it against IN:904 "- **caller runtime check:** Not reproduced: in Chromium, Escape closed only the panel".

  The sheet's runtime premise has three parts. I checked two of them in the code that the build resolves:
  - **The discrete update flushes in a microtask:** `node_modules/.pnpm/react-dom@19.2.7_react@19.2.7/node_modules/react-dom/cjs/react-dom-client.development.js:18985` "scheduleMicrotask(function () {" → :18991 "processRootScheduleInMicrotask();" → :18853 "flushSyncWorkAcrossRoots_impl(syncTransitionLanes, !1);".
  - **The passive effects of a sync commit flush at once:** `node_modules/.pnpm/react-dom@19.2.7_react@19.2.7/node_modules/react-dom/cjs/react-dom-client.development.js:18323` "0 !== (pendingEffectsLanes & 3) && flushPendingEffects();". The Next build of places has the same lines (`node_modules/.pnpm/next@16.3.5_…/node_modules/next/dist/compiled/react-dom/cjs/react-dom-client.development.js:19905` and `node_modules/.pnpm/next@16.3.5_…/node_modules/next/dist/compiled/react-dom/cjs/react-dom-client.development.js:20582` "scheduleMicrotask(function () {").
  - **Resolution surfaces (for `node_modules/.pnpm/react-dom@19.2.7_react@19.2.7/node_modules/react-dom/cjs/react-dom-client.development.js`):**
    - pnpm-workspace.yaml:25-26 "react: ^19.2.7" / "react-dom: ^19.2.7".
    - The overrides at :48-50 cover only esbuild, postcss and undici.
    - :64-65 patches only "'@floating-ui/react@0.27.19': patches/@floating-ui\_\_react@0.27.19.patch", which is the only file in `patches/`.
    - tsconfig.base.json:19-21 maps only "@/*".
    - The `ui` vitest/vite configs have no resolve.alias, and UI holds no vendored react-dom.
    - None of these applies to React. `node_modules/.pnpm/next@16.3.5_…/node_modules/next/dist/compiled/react-dom/cjs/react-dom-client.development.js` is Next's own compiled copy, and no repository entry applies to it.

  The third part is still unverified: a microtask checkpoint between two listeners of one trusted event.
  - The repository records the listener layout that the race needs: `packages/ui/docs/plans/2026-08-26-PDF-VIEWER-HIGHLIGHTS-PLAN.md:333-336` "`useEscapeLayer` listens" / "on `document`, the node React's own root listener sits on under the App Router" … "`defaultPrevented` is the protocol that layer actually honours."
  - Each other inner handler that takes Escape calls `preventDefault`: `packages/ui/src/components/date-picker/use-date-picker-keyboard.ts:100-101`, and `packages/ui/src/components/pdf-viewer/pdf-viewer-highlights.tsx:351-353` "`preventDefault` is" / "what says so: `useEscapeLayer` …". So the missing call is not deliberate.

  The probe names no guard, and I found none. It can decide the claim only if its key was trusted, and the suite has two ways to press a key:
  - `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/setup/act-environment.ts:4-5` "the floating-ui project drives" / "components with real CDP events (`vitest/browser` userEvent)" gives a trusted key.
  - packages/ui/vitest.browser.config.ts:237 "'@testing-library/user-event'," gives a synthetic key, and a synthetic dispatch runs no checkpoint.
  - Also, a Drawer held at a fixed `open` stays open whatever the trace says.
  - IN does not say which of these the probe used.

  So the trace stands and the severity stays high. Q8 asks for the probe that decides.

#### C3-C11

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-picker-trigger.tsx`
- **Symbol:** ColorPickerTrigger
- **Verdict:** CONFIRMED

  - `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-picker-trigger.tsx:65` `const label = hsvaToHex(hsva, alpha).toUpperCase()`
  - `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-picker-trigger.tsx:102` `<span className={cn(k.value, 'min-w-0 flex-1 font-mono')}>{label}</span>`. The span has no `dir` and no isolation.
  - UAX #9 in an RTL paragraph:
    - In "#FF0000", '#' (ET) is next to L. W6 makes it ON, and N2 resolves it to R, so the label shows "FF0000#".
    - In "#00FF00", '#' is next to EN. W5 makes it EN, all seven characters resolve to level 2, and the label shows "#00FF00".
  - So a leading letter or a leading digit decides where '#' goes.
- **Trigger:** as stated.
- **Documented intent:**
  - `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-picker-trigger.tsx:38`
  - `packages/ui/REFERENCE.md:31`: Portal writes "the direction of the nearest direction scope as `dir`", so RTL is a carried mode.
  - The inconsistency is wrong under either answer to Q5.
- **Reach:** none — NONE. Searched `rtl|dir=|direction|DirectionProvider` in all roots. The only hits are Flex `direction` props in the demos and `packages/ui/src/layouts/sidebar/sidebar.tsx:120` `const rtl = useIsRtl()`; no hit renders a picker in an RTL scope.
- **Severity:** low. A reading glitch, and no RTL site.
- **Group:** M11
- **Prior art:** NONE. Searched rtl, dir, bidi, direction.
- **Overturn pass:** **STANDS.** No source pins the label's direction. The closest rule supports isolating it: `packages/ui/src/components/code/code-block.tsx:214` `{/* Code reads left to right in each locale, so an RTL ancestor must not mirror it. */}`, tested at `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/code-block.test.tsx:161`. This feeds Q5.

Chromium and Firefox focus a pressed `tabindex=-1` button. I did not verify WebKit.

For disabled rows, verified in part: the focus move, the lost typing, the lost arrow keys and the lost `aria-activedescendant` context. The same focus move also follows a press on any row that does not complete a click (press, drag off, release).

  - The close comes from the Dialog's escape layer: overlay.tsx`:147-149` `useDismissable({` … `onDismiss: () => onOpenChange(false),` → `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-dismissable.ts:55` `useEscapeLayer({` → `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-escape-layer.ts:66` `if (event.key !== 'Escape' || event.defaultPrevented) return`. There is no composition check. `:70` `dismiss(event)`.
  - The listener is a document bubble listener (`:73`; `packages/ui/src/utilities/document-listener.ts:56` `document.addEventListener(type, reg.listener)`), so it runs after the input's handler.
  - The close then resets the query (state `:162` `if (!open) setQuery('')`).
  - So the defect sits in the escape layer, not in the palette's roving.
- **Trigger:** As stated. Whether a browser reports a composing Escape as `key: 'Escape'` cannot be checked in the repository. `packages/ui/src/utilities/is-composing.ts:11-12` notes that Safari reports 229 with `isComposing` false on a composition key.
- **Documented intent:** `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts:45-47` "Every key of an IME composition also belongs to the textbox: the IME uses Enter to confirm the composition, Escape to cancel it, … Shared with the command palette's search textbox."
- **Reach:** shipped — Shipped default, so every palette input. Sites: place-palette.tsx`:138`, demos.
- **Severity:** low. Only IME users on browsers that report the key are hit, and the cost is a reopen and retype.
- **Group:** M01
- **Prior art:** NONE.
- **Overturn pass:** **STANDS.** `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts:166-169` "the keys of an IME composition belong to the / editable textbox. … and Escape or Enter would close the menu or pick / an option." states the opposite intent for the shared check. `use-escape-layer.test.tsx` has no composition case.

Its dependencies at `:2111` (`[disabled, floating, floatingFocusElement, returnFocusRef, dataRef, events, tree, isInsidePortal, domReference, getNodeId]`) hold no `open`. Focus therefore stays on the confirm button, and a quick second Enter clicks it.

- **Group:** M12
- **Prior art:** NONE. D42's "Confirm toggles from the closure" is the demo checkbox at demos confirm.tsx`:65`, a different seam.

The patch's hunks for this file touch only `useFloatingPortalNode` (`patches/@floating-ui__react@0.27.19.patch:99` `@@ -1532,12 +1532,12 @@ function useFloatingPortalNode(props) {`, `:117`, `:141`), not FloatingFocusManager.

Both input refs keep their identity while the label stays the same: use-scroll-region.ts:142 `[label, labelledBy],` and use-scroll-overflow.ts:159 `[enabled, axis],`.

#### C5-C10

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/code/code-shiki-highlighter.ts`
- **Symbol:** highlightShiki
- **Verdict:** NARROWED
- **Mechanism:**
  - code-shiki-highlighter.ts:174 sets `tokenizeTimeLimit: 0`.
  - @shikijs/primitive@4.5.0 dist/index.mjs:621 `const { tokenizeMaxLineLength = 0, tokenizeTimeLimit = 500, includeExplanation = false } = options;` and :637 `if (tokenizeMaxLineLength > 0 && line.length >= tokenizeMaxLineLength) {`. No length cap applies.
  - @shikijs/vscode-textmate@10.0.2 dist/index.js:1813-1820 `while (!STOP) {` / `if (timeLimit !== 0) {` … `scanNext();`. No time cap applies.
  - code-shiki-worker.ts:10-16: one `onmessage` handler on one thread, with a synchronous `codeToHtml`.
  - code-shiki.ts:71-81 `send` sets no timer, and `fail` (:28-36) runs only from `next.onerror` (:59-63).
  - code-block.tsx:165 together with :197-199 `.finally(() => {` … `else run()` means a pending pass blocks the next one. `loadShiki` (code-shiki.ts:117-122) queues behind it too.
  - Confirmed part: while one tokenization runs, a block that needs a pass keeps its fallback, the highlight of a streaming block stops, and `loadShiki` stays pending. Nothing on the main thread times out or restarts the worker.

#### C5-C15

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/copy-button/use-copy-button-state.ts`
- **Symbol:** useCopyButtonState
- **Verdict:** NARROWED
- **Mechanism:**
  - use-copy-button-state.ts:75 `const timer = setTimeout(() => {` … :79 `}, timeout)` passes `timeout` through unchecked (copy-button.tsx:65 `timeout = 2000,`).
  - Platform behavior: the timer takes a WebIDL `long`. ±Infinity becomes 0, other values wrap modulo 2^32, and a negative result clamps to 0.
  - Confirmed part: `Infinity`, and values from 2^31 to 2^32−1, revert at once.
  - Dropped part: values of 2^32 and above wrap and do not become 0.
- **Trigger:** `timeout={Infinity}`, or a value whose integer part modulo 2^32 is 0 or at least 2^31. The first band is 2147483648 to 4294967296, and each later band has the same form (for example, 6442450944 to 8589934592).
- **Documented intent:** copy-button.tsx:25-26: "Milliseconds the copied state holds before reverting to the rest glyph. @defaultValue 2000". No range is stated.
- **Reach:** none — NONE. No site passes `timeout=` to CopyButton.
- **Severity:** low.
- **Group:** independent
- **Prior art:** NONE.
- **Overturn pass:** **STANDS (NARROWED); trigger and dropped part amended.**
  - Source: the sheet's own rule at C5.md:290: "±Infinity becomes 0, other values wrap modulo 2^32, and a negative result clamps to 0."
  - By that rule, 4294967296 wraps to 0. A value whose remainder modulo 2^32 is 2^31 or more wraps to a negative value and clamps to 0. Both revert at once, so "values of 2^32 and above wrap and do not become 0" is too broad.
  - trigger, restated in full: `timeout={Infinity}`, or a value whose integer part modulo 2^32 is 0 or at least 2^31. The first band is 2147483648 to 4294967296, and each later band has the same form (for example, 6442450944 to 8589934592).
  - verdict, intent, reach, severity, group and priorArt do not change.

### C6: Checkbox, Collapse, and Control

#### C6-C01

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/checkbox/checkbox.tsx`
- **Symbol:** Checkbox
- **Verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **Mechanism:**
  - The Checkbox never asks for `readOnly`. `packages/ui/src/components/checkbox/checkbox.tsx:57-63` reads "} = useControlProps({ / id, / disabled, / required, / 'aria-describedby': ariaDescribedBy, / invalid, / })".

    - `packages/ui/src/components/control/use-control-field-context.ts:52` "readOnly: parent?.readOnly," (the CheckboxField envelope).
  - An explicit prop lands on the native input: `checkbox.tsx:98` "{...props}" with `:99` "type="checkbox"". The native `readonly` attribute has no effect on a checkbox.
  - `checkbox.tsx:88-115` writes no `aria-readonly` and adds no guard against activation.

  - `control.tsx:39-43`: "broadcasts `autoComplete`, `disabled`, `readOnly`, `required`, `severity`, and `variant` / to control-aware descendants. Those are input, textarea, switch, listbox, / combobox, datepicker, checkbox, and radio. Nests: `disabled` / `readOnly` / cascade through inner Controls".
  - `packages/ui/src/components/control/context.ts:54-55`: "Read by input, textarea, switch, listbox, combobox, datepicker, checkbox, / radio."

No audit or plan row and no test covers it (the `__tests__` hits for readOnly are Input, Rating, grid and pickers). The evidence names no guard that the sheet missed.

#### C6-C02

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/checkbox/checkbox.tsx`
- **Symbol:** Checkbox (`indeterminate`)
- **Verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **Mechanism:**
  - `checkbox.tsx:33` "indeterminate," is destructured, so the input markup carries no indeterminate signal.
  - The only writer is a layout effect, which does not run in a server render. `checkbox.tsx:69-72`: "// `indeterminate` is a DOM property with no attribute; sync it before paint. / useLayoutEffect(() => { / if (internalRef.current) internalRef.current.indeterminate = !!indeterminate / }, [indeterminate])".
  - The fill and the glyph key on `:indeterminate`, which only that property sets:
    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/checkbox.ts:21`: "'has-[:indeterminate]:bg-(--check-bg) has-[:indeterminate]:border-(--check-border)',"
    - `:40-41`: "'pointer-events-none absolute stroke-(--check-mark) opacity-0', / '[:checked~&]:opacity-100 [:indeterminate~&]:opacity-100',"
  - So before hydration an unchecked box paints empty.
  - With `checked`, `checkbox.tsx:86` "const Mark = indeterminate ? Minus : Check" and `:106` "checked={resolvedChecked}" show the minus glyph. The native state is still plain "checked".
- **Trigger:** as stated.
- **Documented intent:**
  - `checkbox.tsx:14`: "Renders the partial tri-state: a minus glyph and `indeterminate` DOM property regardless of `checked`."
  - `:69` says "sync it before paint". On a server render the first paint is the server HTML, which the effect cannot reach.
  - The tests cover the client render only: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/checkbox.test.tsx:58-75`.
- **Reach:** shipped —
  - `docs-legacy/demos/components/checkbox.tsx:9` and `:52-54`: the box is indeterminate and unchecked on the first render.
  - The demo is in the prerendered HTML:
    - `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/app/routes.ts:9` "// Each demo file is the route of its page."
    - `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/app/routes/page.tsx:55` "&lt;Outlet />", with no hydration gate.

#### C6-C06

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/checkbox.ts`
- **Symbol:** k (base, `has-checked:border-(--check-border)` / `has-[:indeterminate]:border-(--check-border)`)
- **Verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **Mechanism:**
  - The kata's checked border, `kata/checkbox.ts:20-21`: "'has-checked:bg-(--check-bg) has-checked:border-(--check-border)', / 'has-[:indeterminate]:bg-(--check-bg) has-[:indeterminate]:border-(--check-border)',".
  - The check surface's neutral hover border, joined into the same box through `katakana/control.ts:97`:
    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/control/check.ts:48`: "'not-has-[:disabled]:hover:border-zinc-950/70 not-has-[:disabled]:group-has-[[data-slot=label]:hover]/field:border-zinc-950/70',"
    - `:53`: "'dark:not-has-[:disabled]:hover:border-white/50 dark:not-has-[:disabled]:group-has-[[data-slot=label]:hover]/field:border-white/50',"
  - Specificity:
    - `has-checked` is a class plus `:has(*:checked)`: (0,2,0).
    - The box hover is a class plus `:not(:has(:disabled))` plus `:hover`: (0,3,0).
    - The label hover adds `:is(:where(.group/field):has([data-slot=label]:hover) *)`: (0,4,0).
    - `dark:` adds nothing above that.
  - So the neutral hover border wins in both modes, whatever the source order.
  - The intended checked-hover feedback is the separate rule at `kata/checkbox.ts:22-23` "'not-has-[:disabled]:has-checked:hover:opacity-90',".
- **Trigger:** as stated. A hover on the box alone is enough, so the bare grid checkboxes reach it too.
- **Documented intent:**
  - `check.ts:39-41`: "The resting border is the / only mark of an unchecked control, so it keeps a 3:1 contrast against the / page in each mode (WCAG 1.4.11)."
  - The same seam was already fixed on framed controls. `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/control-validation-hover.test.tsx:9-12`: "Tailwind emits a `dark:` class / after the `has-[[data-invalid]]:hover:` class, and the two have the same specificity. So in / dark mode the gray hover ring replaced the red ring of an invalid control. The neutral hover / now skips each validation state, like the focus ring."
- **Reach:** shipped —
  - `docs-legacy/demos/components/checkbox.tsx:19` "&lt;Checkbox {...props} defaultChecked />": the color axis, checked, in a CheckboxField.

  - `grid-manager-checkbox-row.tsx:32`, which is checked through `grid-column-manager.tsx:349` "checked={!hidden.has(col.id)}" and `grid-group-manager.tsx:597`.
  - The disabled manager rows are excluded by `not-has-[:disabled]`: `grid-column-manager.tsx:294-295` and `grid-group-manager.tsx:683-684`.
- **Severity:** low. It is a border flash on hover only, with no loss of state or a11y, but it shows on shipped grid selection.
- **Group:** independent
- **Prior art:** NONE. I searched the digest for hover, border, dark, checked and check surface. The analogous fix is recorded in a test, not in the digest (quoted above).
- **Dependency:** no node_modules line cited.
  - The selector shapes are Tailwind v4 compile behavior and are not quoted from the resolved build.
  - Tailwind version: `pnpm-workspace.yaml:27` "tailwindcss: ^4.3.0".
  - No override or patch entry names it (`pnpm-workspace.yaml:47-50`, `:64-65`).
- **Overturn pass:** **STANDS. The trace holds over the caller check.**
  - **Selector shapes, now quoted from the resolved build.** From `node_modules/.pnpm/tailwindcss@4.3.0/node_modules/tailwindcss/dist/lib.mjs:16`:
    - `has`: "p.selector=`&:has(${p.selector.replaceAll("&","*")})`"
    - `not`: "`&:not(${L(f,",").map(d=>(d=d.replaceAll("&","*"),d)).join(", ")})`"
    - `checked`: "r("checked",["&:checked"])"
    - `hover`: "i.static("hover",f=>{f.nodes=[q("&:hover",[W("@media","(hover: hover)",f.nodes)])]})"
  - So the checked border is `.x:has(*:checked)` at (0,2,0), and the neutral hover is `.x:not(*:has(:disabled)):hover` at (0,3,0). Source order cannot change the result.
  - **This suite applies that shape on `userEvent.hover`.** `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/control-validation-hover.test.tsx:48` "it('still darkens the ring of a control with no validation state'" runs against `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kasane/layers.ts:45` "'not-has-[>:disabled]:not-has-[[data-invalid]]:…:hover:ring-zinc-400'".
  - **The input carries no border rule.** The element that `getByRole('checkbox')` returns has `checkbox.tsx:111` "className={k.input()}" (`check.ts:24`). A check that read it sees no change. Rerun on the `borderTopColor` of `[data-slot="control"]`, for a box hover and a label hover, in both modes.
  - **dependency:**
    - `pnpm-workspace.yaml:27` "tailwindcss: ^4.3.0".
    - No override names it (`:47-50`: esbuild, postcss, undici).
    - No patch names it (`:64-65`; `patches/` holds only `@floating-ui__react@0.27.19.patch`).
    - `tsconfig.base.json:19-21` maps only "@/*".
    - `packages/ui/vitest.browser.config.ts` has no `resolve.alias`, and `:3` reads "import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'".
    - The store holds one copy, `tailwindcss@4.3.0`.

#### C6-C12

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/control/control-skeleton.tsx`
- **Symbol:** ControlSkeleton (joined silhouette)
- **Verdict:** CONFIRMED
- **Mechanism:**
  - The skeleton picks the joined shape inside any group:
    - `packages/ui/src/components/control/control-skeleton.tsx:36` "const joined = dataGroup !== undefined".
    - `control-skeleton.tsx:43` "className={cn(k.skeleton.base, joined ? k.skeleton.group : k.skeleton.full, className)}".
  - The joined shape is `flex-1` plus a min-width:
    - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/control.ts:24` "base: [rounded.lg, dan.size.control.base]," and `:26` "group: `flex-1 ${dan.size.control.min}`,".
    - `dan/size.ts:83` "base: 'density-h-[7.5,9.5,11.5]'," and `:85` "min: 'density-min-w-[16,24,32]',".
  - A vertical Group is an inline-flex column. `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/group.ts:16`: "vertical: ['inline-flex flex-col [--touch-target-gap-y:0px]', ...tsunagi.vertical].join(' '),".
  - The vertical join classes set no flex value: `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/tsunagi.ts:58-73`, ending at `:73` "'[&>[data-group-orientation=vertical]]:w-auto',".
  - The placeholder is an empty block: `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/placeholder.ts:9` "base: [skeleton.base, 'block density-any:h-4', rounded.lg],".
  - Height: in a column of auto height, `flex-1` gives a basis of 0% against a main size with no fixed value. That basis resolves to the content size, which is 0 for an empty block. The automatic minimum is the smaller of 0 and the specified height, so it is also 0.
  - Width: the members stretch to the max-content width of the inline-flex group, and only the min-width supplies that.
- **Trigger:** as stated.
- **Documented intent:**
  - `control-skeleton.tsx:24-26`: "A skeleton inside a group / therefore draws the joined shape, and one outside draws the full one."
  - `kokkaku/control.ts:7-8`: "`group` / is the in-group default: grows to share the row with sibling / placeholders".
  - Vertical groups are meant to work: `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/control-skeleton.test.tsx:41-52` "it('forwards the stamp, so the join selectors reach the placeholder' … &lt;Group orientation="vertical">".
- **Reach:** test-only — NONE.
  - Searched all five roots for `ControlSkeleton`.
  - `apps/places/src/components/place-filters/place-filters.tsx:32` "loading: () => &lt;ControlSkeleton />," has no Group.
  - `packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/primitives/skeleton.tsx:71`, `:161` and `:164` have no Group.
  - The shipped FiltersSkeleton, which `place-filters.tsx:206` uses, wraps it in a Flex, not a Group: `packages/ui/src/components/filters/filters-skeleton.tsx:45-57`.
  - Test-only site: `control-skeleton.test.tsx:41-53`.
- **Severity:** low. The cost is the shape of a loading placeholder, and no consumer builds the trigger.
- **Group:** independent
- **Prior art:** NONE. I searched the digest for skeleton, Group, vertical, flex-1 and silhouette.
- **Overturn pass:** **STANDS.**
  - **Intent added:** `packages/ui/docs/COMPONENTS.md:80` "It has the box of the real component and takes the same `size`, `level`, or `orientation`."
  - `kokkaku/control.ts:6-9` scopes `group` to a row and gives no vertical form.
  - No browser case covers a vertical group. `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/geometry/skeleton-parity.test.tsx:642` measures a standalone ControlSkeleton only.
  - The zero height is still a reading of the spec, not a browser result.

#### C7-C07

- **File:** `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx`
- **Symbol:** CreditCardInputExpiry

## Root-cause groups

- **M01** (C2:G1, C4:independent): Escape handling is split between the textbox handler and the shared escape layer, which tests neither IME composition nor anything beyond defaultPrevented (use-escape-layer.ts:66), so a composing Escape, or one the combobox consumed without preventDefault, still dismisses the surface. Members: C2-C12, C2-C19, C4-C12.
- **M02** (C2:independent, C4:G4): The highlight seed is an attribute-only, one-shot write (seedVirtualTopMatch, use-a11y-roving.ts:356-373), so the seeded row is never scrolled into view, can be a disabled index, and goes stale when the option set changes under an unchanged query, while the Combobox panel's unstable ref also re-scrolls to the selection on each render. Members: C2-C05, C4-C08, C4-C09, C4-C11.
- **M03** (C1:G1, C7:independent): The server render reads runtime state the browser does not share: outside a LocaleProvider the locale context is empty (providers/locale/context.ts:28-29) and each side falls back to its own runtime locale, and a seeded instant's day is read in each side's zone. Members: C1-C05, C1-C11, C7-C32.
- **M04** (C2:G4, C3:independent): The lock or guard reads only props and the Control channel, and the native Fieldset (fieldset.tsx:13) gives no context, so a disabled fieldset ancestor and an unwrapped path (paste) do not stop custom pointer, key and paste commits. Members: C2-C07, C2-C18, C3-C14.
- **M05** (C1:G2): A standalone calendar hands its grid keys to useA11yRoving's index grid, which wraps at the list ends and has no Page keys, because the date model exists only when a parent steers active. Members: C1-C08, C1-C09.
- **M06** (C2:G2): The open and query setters report every call to the consumer, not only a change. Members: C2-C22, C2-C27.
- **M07** (C2:G3): reanchorOnOptionSwap infers the highlight state from activeIndexRef or a dangling aria-activedescendant and keeps no record of whether the highlight was seeded, moved by the user, or left empty by design. Members: C2-C08, C2-C09.
- **M08** (C3:G1): equalHsva compares rounded HSV channels and an unwrapped hue, not the rendered color. Members: C3-C02, C3-C07.
- **M09** (C3:G2): ColorPicker rebuilds its HSVA from the panel's wire value, and the panel's own HSVA dies on unmount. Members: C3-C04, C3-C09.
- **M10** (C3:G3): useColorPickerState drops part of the cascade it resolves: setTouched from useFormValue and readOnly from useControlProps. Members: C3-C01, C3-C15.
- **M11** (C3:G4): The hex text takes the inherited direction. Members: C3-C11, C3-C12.
- **M12** (C4:G1): A closing modal stays live for its 150 ms exit: Portal keeps the open-time tree, Overlay adds no exit guard, and neither action handler checks for re-entry. Members: C4-C02, C4-C18.
- **M13** (C4:G2): ContextMenu returns bare children when inactive and Menu's contents wrapper when active, so the tree shape and the className target change with disabled and the entry count. Members: C4-C23, C4-C25.
- **M14** (C4:G3): Confirm's header tests truthiness while its default parameter and the ConfirmBody switch test for undefined, and a title-less alertdialog has no name fallback. Members: C4-C21, C4-C22.
- **M15** (C4:G5): List emptiness exists only in CSS, so no state drives the status text or aria-expanded. Members: C4-C04, C4-C05.
- **M16** (C4:G6): k.item styles data-active only through hannou.active, while the forced-colors and glass layers key on :hover and :focus, which a row roved by aria-activedescendant never takes. Members: C4-C14, C4-C15.
- **M17** (C5:G1): A cache-hit render paints from htmlCache without storing a result and the effect returns on htmlCache.has(key), so the block's paint follows the cache and nothing repaints it. Members: C5-C02, C5-C03.
- **M18** (C5:G2): CardHeader and CardFooter pad their inner edge with no sibling condition, so an edge gets two pads when the body is missing or a section sits at an outer edge. Members: C5-C16, C5-C17.
- **M19** (C5:G3): useCopyButtonState tracks no write in flight and no unmount, so a late resolution flips state and fires callbacks, and an unmount cancels the revert with no transition. Members: C5-C12, C5-C14.
- **M20** (C6:G1): Checkbox resolves a fixed set of props and spreads the rest onto the invisible overlay input, so an unrequested cascade value (readOnly) and presentational props (hidden, style) never act on the visible box. Members: C6-C01, C6-C03.
- **M21** (C7:G1): CreditCardInputExpiry infers the edit from the resulting text: the one-digit-month pad also fires on deletions and inserts a meaningful 0 that the caret restore does not count, and the slash guard treats every removal of the trailing slash as Backspace. Members: C7-C01, C7-C02, C7-C03.
- **M22** (C7:G2): The caret's meaningful set is wider than what the format keeps: the masks count every alphanumeric but keep digits, and CurrencyInput counts every '-' but keeps only a leading one. Members: C7-C07, C7-C17, C7-C20, C7-C28.
- **M23** (C7:G3): The card trio has no single default-name policy: expiry and CVV default an aria-label that outranks a native label, and CreditCardInput defaults none, so its placeholder becomes the name. Members: C7-C04, C7-C16, C7-C19.
- **M24** (C7:G4): The expiry's built-in error Message borrows the Field's single error slot for its id and wiring, so outside a Field it has neither and inside one it shares the id with any other error Message. Members: C7-C05, C7-C06.
- **M25** (C7:G5): The currency editor's symbols do not come from the text it reads back: the decimal falls back to '.', the symbols come from the currency style while display and editing use the decimal style, and the locale minus sign is not an editor character. Members: C7-C22, C7-C23, C7-C24.
- **M26** (C7:G6): The masks always restore the caret, and the restore calls setSelectionRange without checking that the input type supports selection, so a type left open to the spread throws on the first keystroke. Members: C7-C08, C7-C18, C7-C21.

### Merge notes

The merge pass wrote these notes on its joins, its near misses, its order, and its file sets. They are quoted as returned.

I made four joins and moved no verdict or severity. My join rule: two groups join when their quoted mechanisms share a file and a symbol, and the shared line carries the cause in both rows. That means it is where the check is missing or the wrong value arises, and at least one scope's step or question puts a fix there. If both rows only pass through a line that behaves correctly, they do not join. Those near misses are listed after the joins.

**M01: C2:G1 (C2-C12, C2-C19) joins C4-C12 (independent).**
- Shared line: `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-escape-layer.ts:66` `if (event.key !== 'Escape' || event.defaultPrevented) return`, in `useEscapeLayer`.
- C2-C12 quotes it as an escape layer with no composition test. C2-C19 quotes `:66-68` as the `defaultPrevented` protocol that the combobox input does not meet. C4-C12 quotes it and concludes "the defect sits in the escape layer".
- C2 S1 already edits this file, and C4 S13 names it as one arm. The two steps become S01.
- C4 Q6 (now Q26) gates only the composition half. Q14 (C2 Q8) is answered "reproduced", so S01 stays first.

**M02: C2-C05 (independent) joins C4:G4 (C4-C08, C4-C09, C4-C11).**
- Shared line: `packages/ui/src/hooks/a11y/use-a11y-roving.ts:373` `setVirtualActive(items, seed(items.length) ? 0 : -1, activeDescendantRef, options)`, in `seedVirtualTopMatch`.
- C2-C05 quotes it for a seeded top match that is never scrolled into view. C4-C11 quotes it as the attribute-only DOM seed. C4-C08 quotes `:358-362` of the same function.
- C4 Q4 (now Q24) already asks for the shared fix. C2 S12 and C4 S10 become S21.
- The combobox-panel.tsx ref half of C2-C05 does not wait on Q24.

**M03: C1:G1 (C1-C05, C1-C11) joins C7-C32 (independent).**

- C1-C05 quotes it, then the per-process fallback at `resolve-locale.ts:11` and `:21`. C7-C32 cites `context.ts:28-30` by range only (I took the text from the repository file), then the Intl runtime default at `use-currency-input-formatting.ts:34`.

- C2-C07 quotes it as "a native fieldset with no context", so the lock, which reads only props and the Control, misses it. C3-C14 quotes it as "a native element" whose `disabled` the panel's prop-only guards miss.

- C2 S6 and C3 S10 become S52.

Both rows quote this line as correctly resolved and then dropped by the consumer. The fixes are in `checkbox.tsx` and `use-color-picker-state.ts`. The other `useControlProps` quotes are different fields: `:87` (C6-C11), `:88` (C2-C16) and `:89` (C2-C07).

**Totals:** 104 scope steps become 100 global steps (four merges), with 60 questions, 26 groups and 137 rows.

**How to read Part B:**
- **Order.** The tiers are:
  - tier 1: high
  - tier 2: medium and shipped
  - tier 3: medium and none
  - tier 4: low and shipped
  - tier 5: low and test-only
  - tier 6: low and none

  Inside a tier, steps with a site in apps/admin or apps/places come first, then ungated before gated, then scope order. Where a scope sheet ordered two steps only because they edit the same file, severity and reach win, and each order string names the move. These dependencies are about the change itself, so they are kept: C1 S1→S5, C1 S2→S3, C2 S3→S13, C2 S4→S5, C3 S1→S3 and S4, C4 S3→S4, C4 S6→S7, and C4 S9→S10→S11→S12.
- **`dependsOn`.** For each concrete file a step shares, I list the nearest earlier step, plus the kept dependencies above. Files that only one answer arm touches still count. A folder entry that ends in `/` means a new test case in a named suite with no named file; it creates no conflict.
- **`files`.** The scope sheet's files, with the overturn changes and every conditional arm, plus the existing test file of any unit the seam names (checked by Glob). What I added:
  - C1 S2: `use-calendar-picker.test.ts`.
  - C1 S6: a new fork-project case under `__tests__/boundary/`, because `vitest.config.ts:297` includes that folder in the fork project.
  - C5 S13: `code-shiki-highlighter.ts`, which the C5-C05 mechanism quotes at `:174`.
  - C6 S1, arm (b): the readers listed at `control/context.ts:54-55`. DatePicker's `useControlProps` calls are in its three state hooks.
  - C3 S6: the panel context and every panel part, for the read-only-parts arm of Q17.
  - S09: the locale provider (`context.ts`, `locale.tsx`) and `providers/locale.test.tsx`.
- **Reach readings that may need your check:**
  - C4-C01 is shipped. Its verified trigger includes the press-without-click path, which the answer places on apps/places.
  - C6-C09 is none. Its answer says the one test site is a client render.
  - C1-C02 is test-only as written. Its intent field, though, says skeleton-parity covers sm, md and lg only.
  - C5-C07 and C5-C12 are none. Their answers say no root site builds the refused copy or the slow write.
- **Q30 (C4 Q10)** gates no step after the overturn. I kept it as an optional design question.

**What I opened:**

##### Part B

## Recommended resolution

The steps run in this order. Two steps whose file sets share a file run in sequence; the others can run in parallel. **Change** quotes the step of the scope sheet as the blind pass wrote it, and the overturn changes to it; the lines above it, which the merge pass computed, supersede the file and order lines inside the quote. Inside a quote, an `S` or `Q` id is the id of its scope sheet; the **Gate** line gives the global question.

The picker serializes once, at its own edge. Keep the channel internal: a new public ColorPanel prop is a public export change (CLAUDE.md §3.1).

    - **S5 (C05), re-derived:**
      - **Change:** end the field edit when the eyedropper is pressed in the picker, so the documented blur commit runs before the pick and the draft clears. The eyedropper escapes the hold for its own press, as the field does at `color-hex-input.tsx:78` `onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}`. `use-color-field.ts` keeps its documented shadow.
      - **Files:** `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-eyedropper.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/color.test.tsx`. The alternative is to limit the hold in `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-picker-content.tsx` to the area and the tracks; that also covers lead 5, which is not a row.
      - **Order:** independent. The old "after S4" reason no longer applies: S4 changes where the pick writes, not where focus sits.
      - **Gate:** none.
      - **§10.3 seam:** an inline ColorPanel inside a wrapper whose `onMouseDown` calls `preventDefault` (the hold, without floating-ui). Stub `window.EyeDropper` and restore it in `onTestFinished` (§10.9). Fire `fireEvent.mouseDown` on the eyedropper and assert that the wrapper's handler did not run. jsdom does not move focus on mousedown, so the focus outcome itself needs the browser suite.

## Open questions

Each question needs the answer of the owner before the steps it gates can run. The axes and what each answer changes are quoted from the scope sheet, with the overturn changes to the question. Inside a quote, an `S` or `Q` id is the id of its scope sheet; the **Gates** line gives the global steps.

### Q01

- **From:** C1 Q1. **Gates:** S09.
- **Question:**
  **Q1 (gates S1): What may the server markup depend on?**
  - **Axes:**
    - (a) Props only. The default locale and a seed's zone-dependent day wait for hydration, as `today` and the clock-seeded month already do.
    - (b) The environment stays readable on the server, and agreement becomes the consumer's duty: an explicit `locale` or LocaleProvider, and seeds built from local parts.
    - (c) A locale fallback that both sides read the same. This answers C05 only.
  - **What each changes:**
    - (a) drops the weekday row, the month label, and seeded days from server markup unless a locale is set. It changes `calendar-hydration.test.tsx:65-75` and `:91-108`, and `use-calendar-month.test.ts:225-229`.
    - (b) leaves the calendar code as is, and S1 adds cases that pin the limit.
    - (c) edits `resolve-locale.ts` or `calendar.tsx` for C05 and leaves C11 to (a) or (b).
- **Answer:** open; back to the owner (the caller was not confident of its recommendation).

### Q02

- **From:** C1 Q2. **Gates:** S35.
- **Question:**
  **Q2 (gates S3): Does a calendar no parent steers carry the date-grid keyboard model, or does that stay with DatePicker?**
  - **Carry it:** S3 adds the date-aware path. Home and End stay at the month ends that `calendar.test.tsx:520` pins (C10 is ruled out).
  - **DatePicker only:** S3 changes no code.
- **Answer:** settled by the owner on 2026-10-05: Carry the date-grid keyboard model in a calendar that no parent steers: month steps at the edges and the Page keys, as the APG date grid does.

### Q03

- **From:** C1 Q3. **Gates:** S77.
- **Question:**
  **Q3 (gates S9): Is `hoverDate` with no `rangeStart` a supported CalendarRange state?**
  - **Supported:** S9 edits `computeRangeDayFlags`.
  - **Parent's duty:** The gate at `use-date-picker-range-state.ts:264` is the contract, and S9 changes no code.
  - **Both:** The selected hover end with a start stays (`calendar-range.test.tsx:90-93`).
- **Answer:** settled by the owner on 2026-10-05: Supported. A hover preview with no `rangeStart` is ignored (no endpoint, no `aria-selected`).

### Q04

- **From:** C1 Q4. **Gates:** S76.
- **Question:**
  **Q4 (gates S5): Does the view seed follow the value cascade?**
  - **(a)** `defaultValue` seeds only an uncontrolled, unbound calendar. A controlled-null or bound calendar becomes clock-seeded, and its month waits for hydration.
  - **(b)** `defaultValue` seeds the view in every mode, and S5 changes no code.
- **Answer:** settled by the owner on 2026-10-05: (a) `defaultValue` seeds the view only for an uncontrolled, unbound calendar (CONVENTIONS §7.2: a bound or controlled value ignores `defaultValue`).

### Q05

- **From:** C1 Q5. **Gates:** S20.
- **Question:**
  **Q5 (gates S6): Which zone defines "local midnight" in `fromCalendarDate`?**
  - **The runtime zone** that every other read uses (`calendar-utilities.ts:27-28`): S6 edits one function, and an app override no longer reaches the calendar.
  - **The library zone:** every Date read moves to it (`toCalendarDate`, `dayNumber`, `monthOf`, `stepMonth`, the Intl formatters, `useCalendarToday`), across three files.
- **Overturn change (C1):**
  **Q5 axes:**
- **Answer:** settled by the owner on 2026-10-05: The runtime zone, which every other read uses.

### Q07

- **From:** C2 Q1. **Gates:** S04.
- **Question:**
  - Q1 (gates S3): what is the re-anchor policy?
    - (a) When rows mount after a filter change that seeded nothing, do we seed the top match on a hover device? Yes closes C2-C09 paths (a) and (b).
    - (b) Does a seeded highlight follow a new top match that mounts above it? "Follow" closes C2-C09 path (c); "stay" leaves it open.
    - (c) Does an empty highlight from a plain open stay empty when the window changes? Yes closes C2-C08.
- **Answer:** settled by the owner on 2026-10-05: (a) Yes, seed the top match after a filter that seeded nothing; (b) a seeded highlight follows a new top match; (c) yes, a plain open stays empty.

### Q15

- **From:** C3 Q1. **Gates:** S53.
- **Question:**
  - **Q1 (S6, C01): when does the picker mark its field touched?**
    - On any panel close: setTouched rides `setOpen`, and the error appears on close.
    - On the trigger's blur, as FormValueResult says ("Call from `onBlur`"). The panel seats focus on its first tabbable (`color-picker-content.tsx:66` `trapFocusProps={{ initialFocus: 0 }}`), so this marks the field on open.
    - The answer decides the handler that carries setTouched and the moment the error shows.
- **Overturn change (C3):**
  - **Q1 (axes refined):**
    - Close arm: DatePicker already does this. `use-date-picker-state.ts:128-129` `// Closing the popover (select, clear, dismiss, or Escape) is the field's` / `// "blur" — mark it touched so validateOn="touched" rules can fire.`
    - Blur arm: it marks the field on open only if the blur is unguarded. Listbox guards it: `packages/ui/src/components/listbox/listbox.tsx:308-309` `// Marks the bound field touched when focus leaves the widget; a blur into the` / `// portaled panel (opening the menu) doesn't count. Mirrors the combobox`, and `:323` `if (refs.floating.current?.contains(next)) return`.
- **Answer:** settled by the owner on 2026-10-05: The picker marks its field touched on panel close (as DatePicker does).

### Q16

- **From:** C3 Q2. **Gates:** S82.
- **Question:**
  - **Q2 (S3, C03): what does the echo guard compare for an HSVA value?**
    - Render-equality, as today: an owner that normalizes counts as an echo, and a controlled change in a hidden channel is dropped. C03 then stands as designed, and the decision refutes it.
    - Equality of the rounded channels: every controlled change is adopted, but an owner that normalizes 360 to 0 snaps the hue thumb on each emission. The hsva part of C07 comes back after S1.
- **Answer:** settled by the owner on 2026-10-05: Equality of the rounded channels, with the hue compared modulo 360 (so 360 equals 0 and the hue thumb does not snap).

- **Answer:** settled by the owner on 2026-10-05: readOnly blocks the open (the DatePicker shape), and it comes from both the Control cascade and a new public `readOnly` prop on ColorPicker, as DatePicker takes it. The reader approved the public prop.

### Q61 and Q62 (from the settle pass)

Applying the answers raised two questions. Q61 is RQ1 and Q62 is RQ2 below, quoted as returned.

**New questions** (they arise from applying the answers; none re-opens a settled question):
- **RQ1 (S97): Radio's read-only signal.** ARIA 1.2 defines `aria-readonly` on `radiogroup`, not on `radio`, and RadioGroup reads no Control (`radio-group.tsx:32` `return <ToggleGroup {...props} role="radiogroup" />`). Choose one:
  - (i) the attribute goes on RadioGroup, which `radio-group.tsx` joining S97;
  - (ii) Radio blocks activation only.
  - The Checkbox and Switch parts can run either way.
- **RQ2: file-order waits.** May a gate-cleared step that waits only through file order on a blocked step run ahead of it? This covers S15, S35, S42 split into its mask/caret half, S49, S50, S64 and S94. Choose one:
  - keep the merge order, so they wait;
  - reorder or split, and the blocked step rebases later.
  - S76 is excluded, because its link is about the change itself.

- **Answer to Q61:** open; back to the owner. The Checkbox and Switch parts of S97 run either way.
- **Answer to Q62:** the caller runs a step that waits on a blocked step only through file order ahead of it; each step is its own commit on one branch, so the blocked step rebases on it later. S76 waits, because its link is about the change itself.

Each scope sheet lists its refuted claims and the parts that a NARROWED or RESTATED verdict drops, with the quoted guard. The overturn changes to each list follow it.

### C1

- **C04 (claim):** No shipped writer puts xs on the root.
  - `packages/ui/src/providers/density/context.ts:30` `} satisfies Record<DensityLevel, InnerStep>`

  - `packages/ui/src/providers/appearance/appearance.tsx:86` `writeRootDensity(root, levelToStep[level])`
  - `packages/ui/src/components/calendar/calendar.tsx:189-191` states the assumption.

### C3

- **C02, the drag trigger at a full CSS pixel.** At every step, one pixel short of the top or bottom edge rounds the value away from 100 or 0:
  - `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-utilities.ts:31` `v: Math.round(clamp(v, 0, 100)),`
  - `packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/dan/size.ts:55` `area: 'density-h-[32,40,48]',`
  - At md (160px), one pixel gives v 99.375 (rounds to 99) at the top and v 0.625 (rounds to 1) at the bottom. sm gives 99.22 / 0.78; lg gives 99.48 / 0.52.
  - The white or black chip checks only within 0.5% of the area height: under 0.8px at md, which is one device pixel on a 2x display. The left-edge part holds at a full pixel (s 0.3 rounds to 0).
- **C04, the rounded `onValueChange` payload on its own,** and a standalone ColorPanel whose display is finer than its wire: both are deliberate per `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-utilities.ts:21-23` (the roundHsva TSDoc quoted in C04).

  - C18: the ui `<Form>` arm. Guard: `use-form-reducer.ts:283` `event.preventDefault()`, with the React gate at `:19062` / `:19084-19086`.

| `hooks/a11y/use-a11y-roving.ts` | The virtual seed does not read `isDisabled` and does not scroll the seeded row. `handleActivationKey` drops the key modifiers. `clearVirtualActive` leaves `data-active` on rows that stay mounted while the panel exits. | C2-C13, C2-C20, C4-C08, C4-C11, C4-C13 |
| `primitives/overlay/overlay.tsx` | The exiting overlay takes input for its whole exit animation; FloatingSurface does not. | C4-C02, C4-C18 |
