### From the verifiers

Each part is the lead list of a blind sheet or an overturn report, as returned. A lead is not a finding: the caller routes an adjacent defect to a sweep and a seam across components to a probe.

#### C1: blind pass

- **L1 (cross-component seam):** `packages/ui/src/modules/chart/engine/chart-time.ts:421` `const date = cursor.toDate(getLocalTimeZone())` uses the same library-zone conversion as C03. An app override would reach the chart time axis. Probe.
- **L2 (cross-component seam):** DatePicker's trigger labels take the ambient locale (`packages/ui/src/components/date-picker/use-date-picker-state.ts:261` `displayValue: value ? formatDate(value, ambient.locale, ambient.dateFormat) : '',`, and `use-date-picker-range-state.ts:250`). If `formatDate` falls back to the runtime locale, a prerendered DatePicker with a value (`packages/ui/src/docs-legacy/demos/components/date-picker.tsx:36` `defaultValue={launch}`) carries C05's seam. I did not read `formatDate`.
- **L3 (cross-component seam):** DatePicker's dialog expects Tab to reach the calendar's controls (`packages/ui/src/components/date-picker/date-picker-content.tsx:137-139`) while it runs a virtual highlight. With C07's missing stop ownership, Tab inside the open dialog may walk every day. Not traced.
- **L4 (adjacent, sweep):** Any `<Name>Skeleton` whose component snaps xs to sm while its silhouette reads a five-value ramp would show C02's gap. Only the calendar was traced.

#### C1: overturn pass

- **L1 widens (cross-component seam):** `packages/ui/src/modules/chart/engine/chart-time.ts:421` `const date = cursor.toDate(getLocalTimeZone())` can also take C03 route (2), not only an app override. `packages/ui/src/__tests__/modules/chart-time.test.ts:310-312` pairs a `TZ` write with `setLocalTimeZone`, while `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/chart-time-zone.test.ts:22` moves `TZ` alone.
- **L3 tie-in:** `packages/ui/src/__tests__/browser/floating-ui/date-picker-input-tab.test.tsx:56-58` and `:80` pin the first and last tabbables of the DatePicker dialog that L3 names. The test checks neither the per-day stops nor their count.
- **Adjacent, for a sweep:** `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/calendar.test.tsx:288` `it('opens a scope at \`sm\` in an \`xs\` scope, because \`sm\` is its smallest step', () => {` and `:298` `it('opens a scope at \`sm\` inside an \`xs\` scope', () => {` render and assert the same case.
- **Adjacent, for a sweep:** `packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/calendar.ts:5` "The `grid`, `day`, and `skeleton` slots are static." But `skeleton: kokkaku.calendar` holds stepped classes (`packages/ui/src/recipes/kiso/kokkaku/calendar.ts:8-9` "Each measure is a stepped / `density-*` class"). S7 edits the same layer.


- I did not open `packages/ui/src/__benchmarks__`, or any `/tmp` path other than the input file.
- One Grep over `packages/ui/docs` spilled its output to a file under `/root/.claude`. I did not open that file and re-ran the searches one doc at a time.
- Searches stayed in `packages/ui/docs`, `packages/ui/src/{__tests__,docs,modules,providers}`, `packages/ui/src/components/{calendar,date-picker}`, and `apps` with `node_modules` excluded. There was one `packages/ui/src` search for a vendored copy, with `!**/__benchmarks__/**`.
- Caller checks: C07 and C14 agree with the trace. C08 records no result.

#### C2: blind pass

- L1 (cross-component seam, for a probe): AddressInput × Combobox highlight anchoring. AddressInput opens the panel only once results arrive (address-input.tsx:193 "open={ready && menuRequested}"). The query therefore changes while the panel is closed, `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox.tsx:559` "lastQueryRef.current = deferredQuery" catches up, and :583 treats the later open as a plain open. The first result set shows with no highlight, and Enter submits the places drawer form.
- L2 (adjacent defect, for a sweep): `packages/ui/src/hooks/a11y/use-a11y-roving.ts:358-362`. seedVirtualTopMatch seeds index 0 of a source without checking isDisabled. This is the filter-change twin of C2-C13.
- L3 (adjacent defect, for a sweep): `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox-panel.tsx:92` "if (event.key === 'Escape') onClose()" closes without preventDefault. With focus in the panel (C2-C04), the same press reaches an enclosing Overlay, as in C2-C19.
- L4 (adjacent defect, for a sweep): a seeded highlight is never scrolled into view (use-a11y-roving.ts:373, compare :605 "if (next) ctx.scrollWithin(next, { block: 'nearest' })"). Together with the open-time scroll to the selection, the top match can sit out of view even with the compiler on (a neighbor of C2-C05).
- L5 (cross-component seam, for a probe): isReservedTextboxKey is shared with the command palette (`packages/ui/src/components/combobox/use-combobox-input.ts:47`). Does the palette's Escape path also close on a composing Escape (C2-C12's class)?
- L6 (cross-component seam, for a probe): `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-controllable.ts:78` reports every set for every control that uses the hook. This is C2-C22's cause outside the Combobox.
- L7 (cross-component seam, for a probe): an implicit submit from AddressInput's input (Enter with no highlight) inside the places Form can leave the panel open while form.tsx:124 disables the fieldset. Whether a click then commits depends on whether the browser blurs the newly disabled input. I did not verify this (C2-C07's class).
- L8 (adjacent, for a sweep): the late keyboardSettled open (C2-C23) sets AddressInput's menuRequested for a field that no longer has focus (address-input.tsx:194). I traced no visible effect.
- L9 (cross-component seam, for a probe): Listbox shares the selection snapshot of `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-deferred-toggle.ts:86`. A controlled open held true there would freeze the same way (C2-C26's class).

#### C2: overturn pass

- **L10 (cross-component seam, for a probe):** `packages/ui/src/components/menu/menu-content.tsx:192-193` "onKeyDown={(event) => {" / "if (event.key === 'Escape') close()" closes the menu with no `preventDefault`. A context menu keeps focus in its panel (:182 "autoFocus={!isDropdown}"). A context menu inside a Dialog is therefore in the class of C2-C19.
- **L11 (cross-component seam, for a probe):** `packages/ui/src/components/menu/use-menu-state.ts:175` "if (!open) clearVirtualActive(triggerRef)" relies on the premise that C2-C20 breaks (use-a11y-roving.ts:113). I did not check whether an Enter on the trigger during the exit clicks a stale `data-active` row.
- **L12 (adjacent defect, for a sweep):** `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox.tsx:410` and `packages/ui/src/components/listbox/listbox.tsx:200` read `defaultValue` to derive `nullable`. CONVENTIONS.md:123 says "A bound field ignores the control's `defaultValue`/`defaultChecked`". A bound field with a stray `defaultValue` therefore loses click-to-clear. This comes from the evidence of C2-C17.
- **L13 (adjacent defect, for a doc sweep):** `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox.tsx:75-76` and :92 promise a hover title for "every selection at all when there is no {@link ComboboxBaseProps.displayValue}". `packages/ui/src/__tests__/components/combobox-utilities.test.ts:305-306` pins no title when there is no resolver (C2-C06).

#### C3: blind pass

1. **Cross-component seam (for a probe).** An open ColorPicker panel portals out of the Form's disabled Fieldset (`packages/ui/src/components/form/form.tsx:124`). `packages/ui/src/components/color/use-color-picker-state.ts:70` resolves `disabled` only from the props and the Control, so the panel stays editable while a Form submits.
2. **Adjacent defect (for a sweep).** ColorPanel reads no Control cascade: `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-panel.tsx:63` takes `disabled` from its prop only, while ColorPicker reads the Control. So `<Control disabled>` leaves an inline panel enabled.
3. **Adjacent defect (for a sweep).** `packages/ui/src/components/control/control.tsx:41-42` lists the control-aware descendants without the color picker, which consumes the cascade.
4. **Adjacent defect (for a sweep).** `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-eyedropper.tsx:53-54` `} catch {` / `// The user dismissed the eyedropper (AbortError); nothing to commit.` swallows every rejection, not only AbortError.
5. **Adjacent defect (for a sweep).** `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-picker-content.tsx:77` keeps focus off every part that does not stop mousedown: the eyedropper, the copy button, and the swatch labels. A pointer press therefore leaves focus on the earlier control. This also feeds C05.
6. **Adjacent defect (for a sweep).** `packages/ui/src/components/color/color-swatches.tsx:59` `aria-label={swatch}` names each chip by its raw hex string.

#### C3: overturn pass

1. Adjacent defect, for a sweep: `packages/ui/src/components/rating/rating.tsx:373` `name={scope.id}` gives the Rating radios an id-scoped name. A Rating in a native form therefore adds an entry under that key, the same mechanism as C18.
2. Adjacent defect, for a sweep: the panel preview has no edge class (`packages/ui/src/recipes/kata/color-panel.ts:41` `const preview = ['relative shrink-0 overflow-hidden', rounded.md, dan.size.colorPanel.preview]`). A white preview on a white surface has no edge, against the edge intent at `:86-87`.
3. Sheet lead 5 now covers only the copy button and the swatch labels. Its eyedropper part is C05's confirmed mechanism (S5).
4. Sheet lead 3 extends: `packages/ui/src/components/control/control.tsx:40-42` also omits Rating, which resolves `readOnly` from the Control (`rating/rating.tsx:142-143`).

#### C4: blind pass

- **L1 (cross-component seam, probe).** Every modal built on Overlay keeps its backdrop (overlay.tsx`:179-192`) and panel live for the 150 ms exit, and keeps keyboard focus inside until unmount (floating-ui.react.mjs`:2086`). FloatingSurface already guards its exit (floating-surface.tsx`:101-107`).
- **L2 (cross-component seam, probe).** The exiting AnimatePresence tree runs the handlers it had when open (floating-surface.tsx`:97-99`), so any Dialog consumer's closed-over guard reads open-time state during the exit.
- **L3 (adjacent, sweep).** `IGNORE_NOTHING` also drops tinykeys's `event.isComposing` guard (tinykeys.mjs`:54`), so ⌘K or Ctrl+K pressed during an IME composition toggles the palette.
- **L4 (adjacent, sweep).** use-keybindings.ts`:19-21` documents `() => false` as the recipe and describes the default as a form-field filter only. With tinykeys 4.0.0, that recipe also drops the repeat and composition guards for every caller that follows it.
- **L5 (cross-component seam, probe).** use-escape-layer.ts`:66` has no composition check, so it affects every dismissable surface that holds a text field.
- **L6 (cross-component seam, probe).** `seedVirtualTopMatch` is shared with Combobox (use-a11y-roving.ts`:333-334`). The `isDisabled` gap and the unscrolled DOM seed apply there too.
- **L7 (cross-component seam, probe).** `handleActivationKey` activates by `node.click()` (use-a11y-roving.ts`:683`, `:688`) for every owner that roves by `aria-activedescendant`, so modifiers are lost in the Menu dropdown and Combobox too.
- **L8 (cross-component seam, probe).** `hannou.active` (active.ts`:12`) has no forced-colors or glass mapping, and the Option kata relies on it (option.ts`:14`).
- **L9 (cross-component seam, probe).** The "no results" `<output>` idiom is shared: `packages/ui/src/modules/grid/grid-column-manager.tsx:61-69` names Combobox and CommandPalette.
- **L10 (adjacent gate gap, sweep).** `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts:29` `const CONSUMER_SPREAD = /^(?:props|rest)$/` cannot see `{...forwardedProps(props)}` (command-palette-item.tsx`:114`, `:124`). That is how C4-C03 passed the gate.
- **L11 (adjacent, sweep).** ChartContextMenu's early returns (chart-context-menu.tsx`:431` `if (contextMenu === false) return <>{children}</>`, and `:437`) switch the tree shape the way ContextMenu`:60` does. Toggling a chart's `contextMenu` therefore remounts the chart.

#### C4: overturn pass

- **L12 (cross-component seam, probe; extends L9).** Combobox has C4-C05's seam: combobox-input.tsx:138 `aria-expanded={open}` and `:139` `aria-controls={open ? controlsId : undefined}` sit beside a listbox that `packages/ui/src/components/combobox/combobox-panel.tsx:41-42` hides with "a `peer/:empty` rule". Whatever Q8 decides applies there too.
- **L13 (adjacent, sweep).** ChartContextMenu resolves a per-target `items` function (`packages/ui/src/modules/chart/engine/chart-context-menu.tsx:238` `() => (typeof items === 'function' ? items({ index: targetIndex ?? null }) : items),`) and forwards `defaultItems` (`:444` `defaultItems={defaultItems}`). With `defaultItems: false` and a function that returns `[]` for some targets, ContextMenu's entries cross zero between right-clicks, so C4-C23's remount reaches the chart body. No root builds this. I did not trace it past `:426-429`.

#### C5: blind pass

1. **Adjacent:** card-body.tsx:4, card-description.tsx:5, card-footer.tsx:5 and card-header.tsx:5 say the slot "accepts `render` for composition". createSlot (core/create-slot.ts:22-27) spreads every prop onto an intrinsic element and handles no `render`.
2. **Adjacent, root of C05:** in a non-default theme, a scrolled overflowing line leaves the `<pre>` box. Its text then sits on the #0d1117 frame.
3. **Cross-component seam:** markdown-renderer.tsx:179 keys each block by token index. An index shift while a message streams remounts the block, which then paints from the cache with no `result`. This is G1's entry path.
4. **Cross-component seam:** observeScrollExtent (observe-scroll-extent.ts:28-34) watches only direct children. Every caller of useScrollRegion or useScrollOverflow whose content changes inside a stable child keeps stale attributes.
5. **Cross-component seam:** the demo smoke `landmark-unique` gate (demo-pages.tsx:94) runs in jsdom with no layout (demo-smoke.tsx:24). No region that exists only on overflow (CodeBlock, Table, ScrollArea, PdfViewer) ever appears to it.
6. **Adjacent (app):** apps/admin/app/(signed-in)/account/two-step.tsx:130 and :232 copy the TOTP key and the recovery codes with no `onCopyError`. A refused write is silent (copy-button.tsx:35-39).
7. **Cross-component seam for S16:** modules/dashboard/dashboard-tile-header.tsx:103 `<div data-slot="card-header" className={cn(k.header)}>` reuses the anchor. A G2 fix that selects `card-header` also matches the dashboard tile header.

#### C5: overturn pass

1. **Cross-component seam (the C11 family).** Two other inline marks also write `data-density` beside their children and open no context:
   - kbd.tsx:20 `<kbd data-slot="kbd" data-density={size} className={cn(k(), className)} {...props}>`
   - text.tsx:35 `data-density={size}`, on PolymorphicStatic with no `density` prop

   But text.tsx:26-28 says its children, "such as a Code or a Kbd, take that step". stat-value.tsx:18 has the same shape. S4 by itself leaves the inline marks inconsistent.
2. **Adjacent (G1).** code-block.tsx:152 reads the module-scope Map `htmlCache` during render (:21 `const htmlCache = new Map<string, string>()`). This is against CONVENTIONS.md:168 "render code must not read a mutable object." S1 keeps that read for the first paint.
3. **Adjacent (component).** color-hex-input.tsx:86 builds its CopyButton with no `onCopyError`, and ColorPanel gives no way to pass one. So a refused hex copy is silent (copy-button.tsx:35-39). This is the same pattern as the sheet's lead 6 and C07.

#### C6: blind pass

- **L1:** Switch does not request `readOnly` either. `packages/ui/src/components/switch/switch.tsx:103-109` reads "} = useControlProps({ / id, / disabled, / required, / 'aria-describedby': ariaDescribedBy, / invalid, / })". This is the C6-C01 seam in a sibling, and `context.ts:54-55` lists switch and radio as readers. Cross-component.
- **L2:** Switch sends `className` to the label (`switch.tsx:116`) and the rest props to the overlay input (`switch.tsx:122` "{...props}"). This is the C6-C03 shape in a sibling.
- **L3:** Radio shares the seams of C6-C05 and C6-C06:
  - the toggle grid (`narabi/toggle.ts:2`, `:15`)
  - the box ramp (`dan/size.ts:36`, "The box of a checkbox and a radio")
  - the hover classes (`check.ts:48`, `:53`)

  Cross-component probe.
- **L4:** Other triggers of held panels. `mount.ts:59-60` reads "the shared lifecycle behind the current-panel cascade, disclosure / panels, and stepper panels". Any trigger that points `aria-controls` at a panel held hidden under `always` has the C6-C09 server-HTML gap. Cross-component probe.
- **L5:** `control.tsx:112` "data-disabled={dataAttr(mergedDisabled)}" follows the merged context. After the deliberate leaf override (`control.test.tsx:206-213`), the wrapper's disabled styling sits on an enabled control. This is next to the refuted C6-C10.

#### C6: overturn pass

- **Radio also never requests `readOnly`:** `packages/ui/src/components/radio/radio.tsx:47` "} = useControlProps({ id, disabled, required, 'aria-describedby': ariaDescribedBy })". This is the C6-C01 seam in a third reader, so it joins L1.
- **Radio builds its `data-disabled` spread by hand:** `radio.tsx:53` "{...(resolvedDisabled ? { 'data-disabled': true } : {})}". `CONVENTIONS.md:72` names `dataAttr` as the home of "A boolean ARIA state or `data-*` attribute".
- **The Accordion panel also clips at rest:** `kata/accordion.ts:71` "panel: ['overflow-hidden', 'contain-inline-size'],". The body padding at `:72` "body: ['px-4 pb-4 pt-0', …" can keep an outline inside the clip. This is the C6-C08 seam; it needs a probe across components.
- **Stepper is a concrete L4 site:** it points `aria-controls` at panels that `mount="always"` holds (`stepper.test.tsx:639-651`, client render only).
- **Input spreads the consumer props after its validation attributes:** `packages/ui/src/components/input/input.tsx:119-120` "{...validation}" / "{...rest}". So a consumer's `aria-invalid` replaces the resolved one. `CONVENTIONS.md:49` puts widget ARIA state after the spread. This is outside C6.


- This pass was not blind, so I read the sweep evidence.
- I did not open `packages/ui/src/__benchmarks__` or any benchmark. A Glob of `packages/ui/*` listed the `vitest.bench*.config.ts` files by name only, and I did not open them.
- Outside the repository, the input file is the only path I opened.

#### C7: blind pass

- **L1 (adjacent defect, the G1 mechanism; route to a sweep):** `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-utilities.ts:87` pads on any non-digit after a leading single digit. A stray letter in "12/2" after the "1" gives "01/22".
- **L2 (cross-component seam; route to a probe):** `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-pending-caret.ts:34-35` calls `setSelectionRange` with no guard for input types that do not support selection. `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts:21-23` allows `type` overrides, so any `useFormattedInput` consumer whose type is overridden that way throws InvalidStateError on the first restore.
- **L3 (cross-component seam; route to a probe):** any two error Messages in one Field share the same id (`packages/ui/src/components/fieldset/message.tsx:61`) and one ref-counted registration (`packages/ui/src/hooks/a11y/use-a11y-scope.ts:115`, :120).
- **L4 (cross-component seam; route to a probe):** `resolveLocale` (`resolve-locale.ts:11`, `:21`) and every Intl call with an undefined locale fall back to the runtime default, which the docs prerender fixes at build time. Other format-aware components on prerendered pages may mismatch as C32 does. Not checked.
- **L5 (adjacent API seam; route to a sweep):** the three card props types omit `type` (`packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-expiry.tsx:18-21`, `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx:22-25`, `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input.tsx:21-24`), yet `packages/ui/src/__tests__/boundary/spread-order-boundary.test.ts:21-23` calls `type="password"` on a `packages/ui/src/components/credit-card-input/credit-card-input-cvv.tsx` legitimate. A consumer can reach that override only through a cast spread.

#### C7: overturn pass

- **L2, narrowed by G6:** what remains is MaskInput. It keeps `type` as a public prop (`packages/ui/src/components/mask-input/mask-input.tsx:49` `type,`, `packages/ui/src/components/mask-input/mask-input.tsx:80` `type={type ?? preset.type}`) on the same `'restore'` path (`packages/ui/src/components/mask-input/mask-input.tsx:66` `} = useMaskInput({`). So `<MaskInput type="number">` throws without any spread. S18 would fix it too. Consumers that use `'jump'` (`packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input.tsx:153`, `packages/ui/src/components/date-input/date-input.tsx:249`) return at `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:61` and do not throw. This is outside C7.
- **L5, amended:** `packages/ui/src/components/mask-input/mask-input.tsx:92-93` says "The props type omits `onChange`, but an untyped spread can still pass one." By the package's own reading, an untyped spread can carry an omitted key without a cast. That applies to the omitted `type` on the card fields and to C30's `onChange`.
- **L6, new (cross-component seam):** the default-label logic in C04 and C16 also appears in other components:
  - `packages/ui/src/components/date-input/date-input.tsx:283` `aria-label={ariaLabel ?? (control?.labelledBy ? undefined : 'Date')}`
  - `components/address-input/address-input.tsx:189` `aria-label={ariaLabel ?? (control?.labelledBy ? undefined : placeholder)}`
  - `components/tag-input/tag-input.tsx:298` `aria-label={ariaLabel ?? (control?.labelledBy ? undefined : (placeholder ?? 'Add tags'))}`

  Q1's answer therefore affects these files, not only the three card fields.
- **L7, new (cross-component seam; the sweep's C12 and C25 evidence names it):** `packages/ui/src/hooks/use-formatted-input.ts:56-70` reformats every change event with no IME composition check. `packages/ui/src/components/currency-input/currency-input.tsx:172` `if (event.key === 'Enter' && !isComposing(event)) event.currentTarget.blur()` checks composition only for Enter. Every mask and CurrencyInput can therefore rewrite the value while an IME is still composing.
- L1, L3 and L4 are unchanged.
